### [1] [RTL-Sequencer: Towards Scalable RTL Timing Prediction with the Sequence-based Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15830)
*Ziyan Guo,Wenji Fang,Wenkai Li,Yuchao Wu,Shang Liu,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了RTL-Sequencer，一种基于序列建模的RTL时序预测新方法，通过线性化逻辑锥和应用现代线性序列模型，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTL精确时序预测是设计自动化领域的长期挑战。现有图方法存在感受野有限、复杂度高、缺乏信号方向性等局限性，亟需新的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于序列的范式RTL-Sequencer。核心技术包括：(1) 通过广度优先遍历线性化逻辑锥；(2) 应用现代线性序列模型；(3) 四种协同技术定制序列模型：序列混洗、双向建模、可微分建模、混合图-序列架构。

Result: 广泛实验表明，RTL-Sequencer相比最先进的基线方法取得了显著改进，推进了早期时序优化。

Conclusion: RTL-Sequencer成功将序列建模引入RTL时序预测领域，为早期设计阶段的时序优化提供了有效的新解决方案，克服了传统图方法的固有局限。

Abstract: Accurate timing prediction at the register-transfer level (RTL) is a longstanding challenge in design automation. Existing graph-based methods struggle with limited receptive fields, high complexity, and a lack of signal directionality. We present RTL-Sequencer, a novel sequence-based paradigm that enables scalable RTL timing prediction via linearizing logic cones by breadth-first traversal and applying modern linear sequence models. Furthermore, sequence models are customized by four synergistic techniques, including sequence shuffling, bidirectional modeling, differentiable modeling, and a hybrid graph-sequence architecture. Extensive experiments demonstrate significant improvements of RTL-Sequencer over state-of-the-art baselines, advancing early-stage timing optimization.

</details>


### [2] [DSTAR: Accelerating Diffusion Transformers via Spatial and Temporal Redundancy Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15846)
*Chi Zhang,Jieru Zhao,Yu Feng,Chen Zhang,Quan Chen,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了DSTAR框架，通过软硬件协同设计加速Diffusion Transformers，在保持精度的同时实现了最高7.33倍延迟加速和41.89倍能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers（DiTs）在图像合成、视频生成等领域广泛应用，但其多迭代推理过程导致性能低效和能耗高。现有加速方法主要关注减少相邻时间步之间的时序冗余，但忽视了DiTs的特定特性，导致要么精度大幅下降，要么无法实现高效率。

Method: DSTAR采用软硬件协同设计方法。算法层面引入细粒度混合精度量化方法，针对线性操作中的差分激活进行优化，显著增加低位计算比例；同时设计稀疏注意力重用机制以最小化注意力层的冗余计算。硬件架构层面设计了专用硬件加速器，在延迟和能耗方面实现高效执行。

Result: 在七个典型DiT模型上的评估表明，DSTAR相比NVIDIA A100 GPU实现了最高7.33倍延迟加速和41.89倍能耗节省；相比现有最先进加速器实现了最高2.54倍延迟加速和3.68倍能耗节省，且无精度损失。

Conclusion: DSTAR通过同时减少空间和时序冗余，成功解决了DiT推理加速问题，在保持精度的前提下显著提升了性能和能效，为DiTs的实际应用提供了有效的加速解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have been widely used in many tasks, including image synthesis, video generation, and content editing. However, their multi-iteration inference process leads to performance inefficiency and high energy consumption. Existing acceleration methods primarily focus on reducing temporal redundancy between adjacent timesteps, but often overlook the specific features of DiTs. As a result, these approaches either suffer from great accuracy degradation or fail to achieve high efficiency.
  We present DSTAR, a software-hardware co-design framework that accelerates DiT inference by reducing spatial and temporal redundancy. At the algorithmic level, DSTAR introduces a fine-grained mixed-precision quantization method for differential activations in linear operations, significantly increasing the proportion of low-bit computations. Additionally, DSTAR incorporates a sparse attention reuse mechanism to minimize redundant computation in attention layers. For architectural support, we design a specialized hardware accelerator which achieves high efficiency in both latency and energy consumption. Evaluation on seven typical DiTs demonstrates that DSTAR achieves up to 7.33x latency speedup and 41.89x energy savings compared to an NVIDIA A100 GPU, and achieves up to 2.54x latency speedup and 3.68x energy savings compared to SOTA accelerators, without accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [3] [From Patterns to Parsers: Automatic Generation of Efficient Hardware Parsers for FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16058)
*Tushar Garg,Andrew Boutros*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种开源工具，能够从高级规范自动生成高效的硬件解析器，生成的解析器在性能和资源效率上显著优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件解析器生成器在表达能力上受限，难以支持复杂的模式匹配操作（如范围验证、否定、外部端口比较），且生成的硬件在性能和资源利用率上存在优化空间。

Method: 提出基于解析中间表示（PIR）的框架，解耦应用特定前端与通用RTL生成后端。后端生成优化的SystemVerilog代码，处理FSM生成、字节对齐及任意数据通路宽度下的多周期字段跨越，并引入自定义符号令牌支持复杂的模式匹配操作。

Result: 生成的以太网解析器相比现有工作，最高工作频率提升226%，FPGA逻辑资源减少97%；分层模式分解相比单片设计资源利用率降低达8倍。

Conclusion: 该开源框架使设计者能够快速实现高性能、资源高效且厂商无关的硬件解析器，适用于以太网协议解析、网络入侵检测等多种应用场景。

Abstract: This work presents an open-source tool for automatically generating efficient hardware parsers from high-level specifications. It uses a parsing intermediate representation (PIR) that decouples application-specific frontends from a common register-transfer level (RTL) generation backend. The backend produces optimized, human-readable SystemVerilog, handling FSM generation, byte-alignment, and multi-cycle field straddling for arbitrary datapath widths. The tool also extends pattern matching beyond simple equality checks by introducing custom symbolic tokens to support operations that existing parser generators cannot express, such as range validation, negation, and comparisons against external ports. We demonstrate two end-to-end flows using a P4 frontend for Ethernet protocol parsing and a Snort frontend for network intrusion detection, both using the same unmodified backend. The generated Ethernet parsers achieve up to 226% higher operating frequency and up to 97% fewer FPGA logic resources than prior work. A controlled synthetic study further shows that the tool's hierarchical pattern decomposition yields up to 8x resource utilization reduction over monolithic designs. Our open-source framework enables designers to rapidly implement high-performance, resource-efficient, vendor-agnostic hardware parsers for diverse applications.

</details>

### [4] [FSZ: Breaking the Prediction-Throughput Trade-off in GPU Lossy Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15413)
*Jiajun Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出FSZ，一种GPU有损压缩器，通过跨块预测状态传递、自适应多阶预测和单次四路评估三种创新，在单CUDA核内同时实现更高压缩比和吞吐率，较cuSZp-P提升10.95倍，较cuSZp-O提升2.92倍，同时保持最高吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有快速GPU误差有界有损压缩器采用纯GPU单核设计实现了高吞吐率，但其压缩比受限，原因是通常在独立块上应用固定的一阶预测器，导致边界残差过多，编码效率低。

Method: 提出FSZ，在预测阶段引入三项互为支撑的算法创新：(1)跨块预测状态传递，将Lorenzo预测状态在256元素瓦片内的块边界间传递，消除8个边界残差中的7个，降低编码率；(2)逐瓦片自适应多阶预测与中心化，自适应选择一阶、二阶或中心化变体作为最佳压缩策略；(3)单次四路评估，利用有限差的数学性质，从单次数据读取中评估所有变体，在相同带宽预算内实现更丰富的预测。整个设计在单CUDA核内完成。

Result: 在NVIDIA GH200 GPU上用8个真实应用数据集实验，FSZ在压缩比上较cuSZp-P最高提升10.95倍，较state-of-the-art的cuSZp-O最高提升2.92倍。同时，FSZ在所有评估压缩器中实现最高平均吞吐率：压缩676 GB/s，解压785 GB/s，无吞吐率损失。

Conclusion: FSZ通过三项预测阶段创新，在保持单CUDA核设计、无吞吐率损失的前提下，显著提升了GPU误差有界有损压缩的压缩比，实现了压缩比与吞吐率的双重领先。

Abstract: Existing fast GPU error-bounded lossy compressors have achieved high throughput through pure-GPU single-kernel designs, but their compression ratios remain limited because they typically apply a fixed first-order predictor on independent blocks. We propose FSZ, a GPU error-bounded lossy compressor that redesigns the prediction stage with three mutually reinforcing algorithmic innovations to achieve both higher compression ratios and higher throughput within a single CUDA kernel: (1) cross-block prediction state carries Lorenzo prediction state across block boundaries within 256-element tiles, eliminating 7 out of 8 boundary residuals that inflate encoding rates; (2) per-tile adaptive multi-order prediction and centering adaptively selects the best compression strategy per tile from first-order, second-order, and centering variants; and (3) a single-pass four-way evaluation exploits a mathematical property of finite differences to evaluate all variants from a single data read, enabling richer prediction within the same bandwidth budget as a fixed predictor. Experiments on NVIDIA GH200 GPU with 8 real-world application datasets show that FSZ outperforms cuSZp-P by up to 10.95x and the state-of-the-art cuSZp-O by up to 2.92x in compression ratio. Notably, these gains come with no throughput penalty: FSZ simultaneously achieves the highest average throughput (676 GB/s compression, 785 GB/s decompression) among all evaluated compressors.

</details>


### [5] [Scalable LLM Agent Tool Access in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15593)
*Mingxin Li,Enge Song,Yueshang Zuo,Xiaodong Liu,Rong Wen,Qiang Fu,Gianni Antichi,Jian He,Jing Tie,Zhou Shao,Xiaobo Xue,Xiong Xiao,Luyao Zhong,Shaokai Zhang,Jiangu Zhao,Jianyuan Lu,Shize Zhang,Xiaoqing Sun,Changgang Zheng,Zihao Fan,Haonan Li,Tian Pan,Xiaomin Wu,Yang Song,Xing Li,Biao Lyu,Meng Li,Haipeng Dai,Guihai Chen,Shunmin Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了云规模MCP网关系统，解决LLM Agent工具调用的可扩展性、兼容性和性能问题，支持3000+工具访问，显著降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent依赖工具调用，MCP成为事实标准接口，但在云规模部署中面临挑战：工具提供方需适配遗留服务和协议兼容性成本；Agent端受限于上下文窗口，大规模工具集增加token开销和推理延迟，降低任务成功率；有状态MCP后端的会话保持增加客户端复杂度。

Method: 设计云规模MCP网关系统，打破数据面直连模型。网关层统一卸载遗留服务集成、MCP变体兼容、访问控制、工具推荐、会话感知路由。采用混合检索技术进行工具推荐。

Result: 混合检索达到98% Top-15召回率；支持Agent扩展访问3000+工具，工具选择准确率高；工具选择时间减少8.9倍，token使用量减少23.8倍；单次调用开销低，横向扩展稳定性好。

Conclusion: 网关系统成功解决了MCP云规模运营难题，实现了工具推荐高效性与准确性的平衡，并分享了生产部署经验教训，为LLM Agent工具调用基础设施提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on tool calling to act on external systems, and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) has quickly become its de facto interface. Operating MCP at cloud scale, however, becomes difficult. On the tool provider side, legacy services are not directly callable through MCP; the rapid protocol development also creates ongoing compatibility cost. On the agent side, the number of accessible tool is limited by the LLM context window and inference overhead; mounting a large tool set increases token usage and inference latency and can reduce task success rate. Moreover, for stateful MCP backends with multiple replicas, preserving session affinity increases client-side complexity.
  We present a cloud-scale gateway system for MCP service. It breaks the direct-connect model on the data plane and offloads legacy service integration, consolidating incompatible MCP variants, access control, tool recommendation, and session-aware routing to the gateway. Hybrid retrieval sustains 98% Top-15 recall; it scales agent tool access to 3,000+ with high tool selection accuracy, and reduces tool selection time by $8.9\times$ and token usage by $23.8\times$, with low per-call overhead, stable under scale-out. Finally, we share the lessons learned from deploying the gateway system in production.

</details>


### [6] [JoyNexus: Service-Oriented Multi-Tenant Post-Training for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16074)
*Haoran Sun,Wentao Zhang,Junyang Hua,Hedan Yang,Yongjian Guo,Yifei Zhang,Xiaolong Xiang,Mingxi Luo,Jing Long,Chen Zhao,Chen Zhou,Wanting Xu,Qiming Yang,Hui Zhang,Song Wang,Xiaodong Bai,Shuai Di,Xu Chu,Xiaotie Deng,Yicheng Gong,Junwu Xiong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了JoyNexus，一个面向多租户VLA模型后训练的统一服务平台，通过解耦服务组件、共享基础模型和组批处理技术，有效降低了GPU时间并提升了资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型后训练计算服务通常为单租户分配独占的GPU和CPU资源，这种模式虽然最大化了客户端灵活性，但给用户带来了基础设施适应负担，同时固定的卡时计费模型使得短期或突发性工作负载对租户来说成本高昂，对服务提供商来说效率低下。

Method: JoyNexus解耦了训练模型服务、推理模型服务和环境服务，每个服务通过API访问，并由常驻共享基础模型和租户特定槽位支持。引入全局训练队列和推理队列进行多租户调度，并提出组批处理技术，使具有兼容模型前缀的异构VLA数据模式能够共享骨干网络前向传递。

Result: 在真实具身场景中通过工作负载模拟和组批处理管道评估表明，与隔离的单租户执行相比，JoyNexus减少了总体GPU时间，并通过共享资源上的跨租户调度提高了服务利用率。

Conclusion: JoyNexus成功解决了多租户VLA后训练场景下的资源效率问题，为VLA模型的监督微调、强化学习和评估提供了统一高效的服务平台。

Abstract: The post-training of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential due to the diversity of simulators, robot embodiments, and task objectives. Existing compute services, whether offered as direct accelerator rental or batch-workload submission, typically allocate an exclusive set of GPU and CPU resources to a single tenant. While this paradigm maximizes client flexibility, it burdens users with infrastructure adaptation, and the fixed card-hour accounting model renders short or bursty workloads both expensive for tenants and inefficient for the service provider. To address these challenges, we present JoyNexus, a unified service for multi-tenant VLA supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and evaluation. JoyNexus decouples the Training Model Service, Inference Model Service, and Environment Service, each accessed through APIs and backed by resident shared base models with tenant-specific slots. Tenants can directly invoke high-level semantic APIs for training, rollout, and evaluation, or compose custom algorithms using lower-level APIs and their assigned endpoints. Multiple tenants submit workloads concurrently; their action modules, optimizers, rollout records, and policy versions remain isolated, and the service is scheduled by the global Training Queue and Inference Queue. To further improve multi-tenant training efficiency, JoyNexus introduces group batching for heterogeneous VLA data schemas that share a compatible model-facing prefix, enabling a single shared backbone forward pass over grouped samples. Finally, we evaluate JoyNexus through workload simulation and a group-batching pipeline in a realistic embodied scenario. Results show that, compared with isolated single-tenant execution, JoyNexus reduces aggregate GPU time and improves service utilization via cross-tenant scheduling on shared resources.

</details>


### [7] [Every Microsecond Matters: Achieving Near Speed-of-Light Latency in GPU Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16100)
*Siyuan Shen,Anton Korzh,John Bachan,Tiancheng Chen,Arnav Goel,Ludwig Schneider,Pouya Kousha,Zhenhao He,Sylvain Jeaugey,Kamil Iskra,Nishank Chandawala,Jeff R. Hammond,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了低延迟GPU集合通信优化方法，通过屏障自由同步和对称内存/多播技术，将小中消息延迟降低至硬件光速下界的7%以内，显著提升了LLM推理和HPC应用的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU集合通信主要针对带宽优化，但新兴工作负载（如长上下文解码密集型LLM推理）越来越受延迟限制。在多GPU服务大模型场景下，许多小规模集合操作直接位于token生成的关键路径上，即使微秒级的开销也会显著影响性能和成本。

Method: 识别了近优设计的关键原则：屏障自由同步、对称内存和多播的高效利用。基于NCCL的设备端API开发了低延迟接口来构建自定义集合内核，并在NCCL中实现了新的对称集合操作。

Result: 微基准测试显示小中消息延迟显著降低，开销降至绝对硬件光速下界的7%以内。集成到实际应用后，改善了LLM推理的token间延迟和吞吐量，并加速了cuSOLVERMp。

Conclusion: 所提出的低延迟集合通信优化方法成功逼近硬件性能极限，在AI推理和传统HPC工作负载中均展现出显著效益，证明了该方法的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: GPU collective communication is typically optimized for bandwidth, yet many emerging workloads are increasingly limited by latency. Long-context decode-heavy large language model (LLM) inference is a prime example, where serving large models requires multiple GPUs, and many small collectives lie directly on the critical path of token generation. Therefore, even microsecond of overhead can impact performance and cost. In this work, we study how to approach the hardware Speed-of-Light (SoL) lower bound for GPU collectives within a scale-up network. We identify key principles for near-optimal designs, including barrier-free synchronization and efficient use of symmetric memory and multicast. Building on NCCL's device-side API, we develop low-latency interfaces for constructing custom collective kernels and use them to implement new symmetric collectives in NCCL. Microbenchmarks show substantial latency reductions for small and medium messages, reducing overhead to within 7% of the absolute SoL lower bound. When integrated into real applications, these kernels improve inter-token latency and throughput in LLM inference and accelerate cuSOLVERMp, demonstrating benefits for both AI inference and traditional HPC workloads.

</details>

### [8] [GraphDx: A Cost-Aware Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Agent Framework for Sequential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15280)
*Shaoting Tan,Ning Liu,Yuntao Du,Shuyue Wei,Wu Shuai,Qian Li,Yanyu Xu,Wei Zhang,Lizhen Cui,Haitao Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GraphDx框架，通过构建医学诊断知识图谱(MDKG)和三个协作Agent来弥合LLM在成本约束下的知识-推理差距，在提升诊断成功率的同时显著降低检查成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法在序列诊断任务中存在关键的"知识-推理差距"：尽管编码了大量医学知识，但在成本约束下难以进行系统性推理，往往过度检查，无法平衡诊断准确性与资源成本。

Method: 提出GraphDx知识增强框架，包含两大创新：(1)自动化流程构建医学诊断知识图谱(MDKG)，具有量化典型性、行动中心拓扑和双重目标属性；(2)设计三个协作Agent(感知、推理、决策)，感知和决策Agent处理语言理解与生成，推理Agent在MDKG上执行确定性证据评分和成本感知规划。

Result: 在MedQA和MIMIC-IV数据集上，使用三种LLM骨干(DeepSeek-V3、Kimi-k2、Llama-3.3)进行实验，诊断成功率从50-68%提升至79-93%，同时检查成本降低20-54%。

Conclusion: GraphDx为自动化临床诊断提供了一个稳健、经济且可解释的解决方案，有效弥合了LLM的知识-推理差距，实现了诊断准确性与资源成本的平衡。

Abstract: Sequential diagnosis requires balancing diagnostic accuracy against resource costs through iterative information gathering. Existing Large Language Model (LLM) approaches exhibit a critical knowledge-reasoning gap: despite encoding extensive medical knowledge, they struggle to reason systematically under cost constraints, often resorting to excessive testing. We propose GraphDx, a knowledge-enhanced framework with two core innovations. First, we design an automated pipeline that leverages LLMs to construct Medical Diagnosis Knowledge Graphs (MDKGs) with quantized typicality, action-centric topology, and dual-objective attributes for both diagnostic relevance and cost-sensitivity. Second, we introduce three collaborative agents (Perception, Reasoning, and Decision) where the Perception and Decision Agents handle language understanding and generation, while the Reasoning Agent performs deterministic evidence scoring and cost-aware planning on the MDKG. Experiments on MedQA and MIMIC-IV across three LLM backbones (DeepSeek-V3, Kimi-k2, Llama-3.3) show that GraphDx improves diagnostic success rates from 50--68% to 79--93% while reducing test costs by 20--54%, providing a robust, economical, and interpretable solution for automated clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [9] [Causal-Audit: Explicit and Auditable Graph-based Reasoning via Target-Aware Causal Chain Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15281)
*Su Lan,Xuefei Yin,Yanming Zhu,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个显式且可审计的因果推理框架，用于无语境干预问答任务，通过目标感知的因果图构建策略和路径级证据聚合机制，实现了比现有LLM方法更优的性能和更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的因果和干预问答方法依赖于隐式的语言级推理，导致因果假设不透明、推理路径不可验证，且在复杂干预下预测脆弱，尤其是在无语境设置中表现不佳。

Method: 提出显式可审计的因果推理框架，将因果推理形式化为在显式因果图上的结构化推理，包含四个模块化阶段。关键创新包括：(1)目标感知的因果图构建策略，将目标变量作为图扩展核心约束，抑制无关变量和虚假因果关系；(2)路径级因果证据聚合机制，结合多条因果路径并建模强化和对抗效应，实现超越单链推理的鲁棒决策。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该框架持续优于现有的基于LLM的方法，同时提供可解释和可审计的因果推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过显式因果图构建和路径级证据聚合，该框架成功解决了现有LLM因果推理方法的不透明和脆弱性问题，为无语境干预问答任务提供了更可靠、更可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Causal and intervention-based question answering is fundamental to advancing large language models (LLMs) toward reasoning beyond surface-level correlations and understanding underlying causal mechanisms. However, existing LLM-based methods often rely on implicit language-level reasoning, resulting in opaque causal assumptions, unverifiable reasoning paths, and fragile predictions under complex interventions, particularly in context-free settings. In this paper, we propose an explicit and auditable causal reasoning framework for context-free intervention-based question answering. Our method formulates causal inference as structured reasoning over an explicit causal graph through four modular stages, rather than implicit end-to-end prediction. A key innovation is a target-aware causal graph construction strategy that treats the target variable as a core constraint during graph expansion, effectively suppressing irrelevant variables, spurious causal relations, and reasoning noise. We further introduce a path-level causal evidence aggregation mechanism that combines multiple causal paths while modeling both reinforcing and counteracting effects, enabling robust decision-making beyond single-chain reasoning. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms existing LLM-based methods while providing interpretable and auditable causal reasoning traces.

</details>


### [10] [Cura 1T: Specialized Model for Agentic Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15314)
*actAVA AI,:,Haolin Chen,Leon Qi,Steve Brown,Deon Metelski,Tao Xia,Joonyul Lee,Qixuan Wang,Kevin Riley,Frank Wang,Weiran Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Cura 1T，一个通过人类把关的自进化循环训练的医疗专用LLM，在医疗评估套件中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域涵盖高风险沟通、专家推理和工作流执行，但能够同时覆盖这些用例的专门化LLM仍然有限。医疗模型需要处理患者咨询、文本和图像的临床推理、交互式诊断以及电子健康记录(EHR)工具使用。这些能力以不同方式失败，针对一个任务的狭窄更新可能降低另一个任务的表现。

Method: 采用人类把关的自进化循环训练方法。在每个进化轮次中，训练代理规划目标能力、训练模型、评估基准轨迹，并根据观察到的失败优化数据混合。这种以数据为中心的循环通过针对性的合成和精心筛选的示例来改进模型，而非单一的通用医疗数据更新。

Result: 在医疗评估套件中，Cura 1T在前沿基线中排名靠前或接近顶部，同时在域外推理和智能体基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过人类把关的自进化循环方法能够有效训练医疗专用LLM，以数据为中心的针对性优化比单一通用医疗数据更新更有效，成功实现了多能力协同提升。

Abstract: Healthcare spans high-stakes communication, expert reasoning, and workflow execution, yet specialized LLMs that cover these use cases together remain limited. A healthcare model must handle patient consultation, clinical reasoning over text and images, interactive diagnosis, and electronic health record (EHR) tool use. These capabilities fail in different ways, and a narrow update for one task can degrade another. We present Cura 1T, a healthcare-specialized LLM trained through a human-gated self-evolution loop. In each evolution round, a training agent plans a target capability, trains the model, evaluates benchmark trajectories, and refines the data mixture from observed failures. This data-centered loop improves the model through targeted synthetic and curated examples rather than a single generic medical-data update. Across the healthcare evaluation suite, Cura 1T ranks at or near the top among frontier baselines, while remaining competitive on out-of-domain reasoning and agentic benchmarks.

</details>


### [11] [AnovaX: A Local, Multi-Agent Voice Assistant with LLM Planning, Typed Executors, and Adaptive Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15367)
*Raunak B Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AnovaX，一个完全在本地运行的桌面语音助手系统，通过Python进程集成唤醒词检测、语音处理、LLM规划器、安全层、多智能体编排器和恢复循环，实现了对桌面应用的自动化控制，并支持通过手机远程监控和操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的桌面语音助手（如Siri、Alexa）主要依赖云端管道，需要将原始音频上传至云端，且仅暴露固定的技能集。作者希望构建一个完全本地优先的助手系统，将桌面本身作为操作界面，避免云端依赖，同时提供更灵活的可扩展性。

Method: AnovaX采用单一Python进程架构，集成以下组件：唤醒词门控、语音管道、Gemini LLM规划器（生成JSON工具调用计划）、白名单/黑名单安全层、多智能体编排器（在受限线程池上生成类型化子智能体）、自适应恢复循环。系统包含9个专用智能体类（如AppAgent、TypingAgent、BrowserAgent等），每个具有独立的超时、重试策略和共享资源锁。递归MetaAgent允许规划器将子目标委托回自身（最多两级嵌套）。恢复循环使用ReAct风格提示，并通过推测性执行只读工具隐藏Gemini的延迟。配套Flask服务器提供本地WiFi上的手机友好远程控制，实时镜像智能体生命周期事件，并通过MJPEG流式传输笔记本屏幕。

Result: AnovaX成功实现了打开应用、输入文本、运行搜索、协调并发操作、从单步失败中恢复，以及从另一个房间的手机完全驱动等功能——所有这些都在LLM不直接接触键盘的情况下完成。系统展示了仅需几千行代码的清晰助手即可实现上述功能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了构建一个可读、简洁（几千行代码）的本地语音助手系统是可行的，该系统能够在完全本地环境中实现应用控制、并发协调和故障恢复，并通过手机远程界面进行监控和操作，为本地优先的桌面助手设计提供了一个实用的参考实现。

Abstract: Desktop voice assistants are still dominated by cloud pipelines that ship raw audio off the machine and expose a fixed set of skills. We describe AnovaX, a small local-first assistant that runs entirely on the user's computer and treats the desktop itself as its action surface. A single Python process wires together a wake-word gate, a speech pipeline, an LLM planner (Gemini) that emits a JSON plan of tool calls, a whitelist-and-denylist safety layer, a multi-agent orchestrator that translates each plan into typed child agents on a bounded thread pool, and an adaptive recovery loop that takes over whenever a core step fails. Every tool corresponds to a specialized agent class (AppAgent, TypingAgent, BrowserAgent and six others) with its own timeout, retry policy, and shared-resource locks. A recursive MetaAgent lets the planner delegate a sub-goal back to itself, capped at two levels of nesting. The recovery loop uses a compact ReAct-style prompt and hides Gemini's latency behind speculative execution of read-only tools. A companion Flask server exposes a phone-friendly remote over the local WiFi, mirrors every agent lifecycle event to the phone in real time, and streams the laptop's screen back over MJPEG so the user can watch remote commands land as they run. The point of the project is less to compete with Siri or Alexa than to show that a legible, few-thousand-line assistant is enough to open apps, type into them, run searches, coordinate concurrent actions, recover from single-step failures, and be driven entirely from a phone in another room -- without the LLM ever touching the keyboard.

</details>


### [12] [AgentFAIR: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for FAIRness Evaluation of Geospatial Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15781)
*Ming Chen,Pranav Pai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有FAIR评估工具不一致的问题，提出了AgentFAIR多智能体框架，结合结构化元数据提取和13个子原则LLM评估器，实现了更高的评估一致性和专家对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间数据集需要符合FAIR原则，但现有评估工具使用不同的评分标准和证据来源，在JavaScript渲染页面或特定存储库标识符上可能失败，导致评估结果不一致（标准差达15.0个百分点）。

Method: 提出AgentFAIR多智能体框架，结合结构化元数据提取与13个子原则特定的LLM评估器，每个评估器生成0-3成熟度分数、引用证据和建议；引入批评智能体检查证据和一致性，可请求针对性重新评估。

Result: 可发现性、可访问性、互操作性和可重用性平均分数分别为79.7%、70.4%、45.3%和72.0%；子原则一致性平均89%；专家研究Fleiss's kappa为0.71，与专家共识对齐82%；API成本约0.054美元/数据集。

Conclusion: 结果支持可审计性和可行性，但有限的基准测试、不完整的消融实验和单模型家族验证限制了对准确性和泛化性的声明。

Abstract: Geospatial datasets support applications from urban planning to climate modeling, yet consistent assessment of FAIR compliance is difficult. Existing evaluators use different rubrics and evidence sources and may fail on JavaScript-rendered pages or repository-specific identifiers. For 50 datasets from 10 repositories, the standard deviation of normalized scores across available tools averages 15.0 percentage points and reaches 30.3 for one dataset. Because these outputs are not equivalent measurements, we use them to characterize disagreement and failure modes, not comparative accuracy. We present AgentFAIR, a multi-agent framework combining structured metadata extraction with 13 sub-principle-specific LLM evaluators. Each produces a 0-3 maturity score, cited evidence, and recommendations; a critic checks evidence and consistency and can request targeted re-evaluation. Mean Findability, Accessibility, Interoperability, and Reusability scores are 79.7%, 70.4%, 45.3%, and 72.0%. Rank correlations with four baseline tools range from 0.31 to 0.61; the FAIR-enough comparison is not statistically significant. On a 10-dataset repeated-run subset, sub-principle agreement averages 89% (standard deviation: 3 percentage points), versus 71% without the critic. A preliminary 15-dataset expert study yields Fleiss' kappa of 0.71 and 82% alignment with expert consensus. API cost is approximately USD 0.054 per dataset. These results support auditability and feasibility, while the limited benchmark, incomplete ablations, and single-model-family validation constrain claims about accuracy and generalization.

</details>


### [13] [Precise but Uncoupled: Reviewer Precision Does Not Guarantee Critique Uptake in Multi-Agent Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15388)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Jingyan Jiang,Vikram Vasudevan,Cheng-Hau Yang,Huihuo Zheng,Le Chen,Eliu A. Huerta,Venkatram Vishwanath,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在数学和科学Agent系统中，专门化的审阅者角色并不如预期有效。虽然审阅者能精准发现错误，但如果系统不能有效利用审阅意见进行修正，最终问题解决率并不会提高。广播式同伴讨论在困难问题上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多面向数学和科学的Agent系统采用层次化设计，包含专门的审阅者角色，假设专用的审阅阶段能帮助将错误候选转变为正确答案。作者希望验证这一假设是否成立，探究审阅者质量是否能真正提升系统的问题解决能力。

Method: 在4,181个经过验证的Omni-MATH问题上进行实验，使用匹配的gpt-oss-120b作为执行器。比较了两种协议：规划器-执行器-审阅者管道（PER）与广播式同伴讨论。通过分析审阅者精度、批评意见采纳率以及审阅引导的修复效果，探究性能差距的原因。还测试了显式确认机制和将审阅指导嵌入求解器工作上下文两种干预措施。

Result: 在简单层级上协作收益有限；从第4层开始，广播式同伴讨论的最终准确率显著高于PER管道。尽管PER的审阅者精度更高（0.861 vs 0.644），但经验证有用的批评意见更不可能改变下一个候选答案，导致较低的审阅引导修复率。审阅者检测质量与批评采纳率在经验上是可分离的。强制显式确认会降低最终准确率；将审阅指导嵌入求解器上下文部分改善了执行效果但未能消除差距。

Conclusion: 以审阅者为中心的评估可能高估系统质量：一个协议可能擅长发现错误，但如果不能有效利用这些批评意见进行修正，仍无法解决更多问题。系统设计应关注批评意见的实际采纳和执行，而非仅仅关注错误检测能力。

Abstract: Many math- and science-oriented agent systems use hierarchical designs with specialized reviewer roles, assuming that a dedicated review stage should help turn wrong candidates into correct ones. We test this assumption on 4,181 verifier-grounded Omni-MATH problems using matched gpt-oss-120b actors. Collaboration adds little on the easiest tiers, but from tier 4 onward the gains open sharply; in this harder regime, broadcast-style peer discussion reaches higher final accuracy than a planner-executor-reviewer pipeline (PER). We ask whether this gap is explained by reviewer quality or by whether critique changes the next answer the protocol carries forward. It is not explained by reviewer precision alone: PER's reviewer is more precise than broadcast's (0.861 vs. 0.644), yet evaluator-verified useful critique is much less likely to change the next candidate and produces lower reviewer-guided repair. These results show that reviewer detection quality and critique uptake are empirically separable. Within matched PER interventions, forcing explicit acknowledgment lowers final accuracy, while embedding reviewer guidance directly in the solver's working context partially improves follow-through without closing the gap. Overall, reviewer-centric evaluation can overstate system quality: a protocol may spot errors well yet still fail to solve more problems if it does not act on those critiques.

</details>


### [14] [DrawingVQA: A Real-World Benchmark for Multi-Depth Visual-Textual Reasoning on Construction Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15418)
*Yoonhwa Jung,Junryu Fu,Mani Golparvar-Fard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DrawingVQA，首个针对建筑工程图纸的多模态大语言模型评测基准，包含33张实际施工图纸和92个专家标注问答对，评估发现现有模型在高层次推理能力上与专家水平存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态评测基准主要针对自然图像或简化示意图，而建筑工程图纸融合了几何图形、符号标记、表格数据、注释和专业文本等复杂元素，是工程实践中的核心媒介。缺乏专门针对这一重要领域的评测基准阻碍了AI在工程领域的应用发展。

Method: 构建了DrawingVQA评测基准，包含33张"用于施工"的实际工程图纸和92个专家精心标注的问答对。设计了三种推理深度层次：感知理解、情境解释和领域专家推理。提出了双重分类框架，联合分析七个建筑工程维度和四个MLLM能力维度的表现。

Result: 对最先进的多模态大语言模型进行评估，发现模型表现与专家水平之间存在显著差距，尤其在更高层次的推理深度上差距更为明显。这是首个明确将工程工作流程映射到AI推理能力的研究。

Conclusion: DrawingVQA为领域专业化的多模态推理研究奠定了基础，有助于推动AI驱动的理解能力与实际工程工作流程的融合，为未来在建筑、土木等工程领域开发专用AI模型提供了重要的评测工具。

Abstract: We introduce DrawingVQA, the first benchmark designed to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on real-world construction drawings -- a core media in architecture, civil, and many other engineering practices. Unlike natural images or schematic floor plans, construction drawings fuse abstract geometry, symbolic notation, tabular data, annotations, and domain-specific text, forming a uniquely complex visual-textual domain core to engineering workflows. DrawingVQA bridges this gap with 33 "Issued for Construction" drawings and 92 expertly curated question-answer pairs, spanning three reasoning depths: perceptual understanding, contextual interpretation, and domain-expert reasoning. To evaluate model capabilities, we present a dual categorization framework to jointly analyze performance across seven construction-engineering and four MLLM capability dimensions -- the first to explicitly map engineering workflows to AI reasoning competencies. Evaluations of state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal a substantial gap between model and expert performance, particularly at higher reasoning depths. This benchmark lays a foundation for domain-specialized multimodal reasoning to allow for advancement on integration of AI-driven understanding and real-world engineering workflows.

</details>


### [15] [Do Coding Agents Need Executable World Models, Simplification, and Verification to Solve ARC-AGI-3?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15439)
*Sergey Rodionov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过消融实验解构了ARC-AGI-3智能体的性能来源，发现验证机制贡献最大，简化策略部分有效，而强制执行性世界模型并非总是有益；在最强模型上验证变体成功饱和了公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究团队之前的ARC-AGI-3智能体集成了执行性世界建模、计划性简化和精确回放验证三个组件，但各组件对性能的贡献程度不明确。为回答这一归因问题，需要设计消融实验来分离和评估每个组件的独立贡献。

Method: 设计四个嵌套的Codex智能体变体进行对比实验：（1）纯文本基线；（2）带灵活接口的执行性世界模型（无回放验证）；（3）执行性世界模型+计划性简化；（4）固定接口验证处理（含简化，需精确再现记录的观测）。使用gpt-5.4和gpt-5.5在高低两个推理努力水平上评估所有变体，并用gpt-5.6-sol进行探索性后续研究。

Result: 核心发现：（1）所有变体均随模型增强和推理努力增加而提升；（2）变体间差异小于预期，组件效应随设置变化；（3）持久性执行产物并非普适有益——文本变体在gpt-5.5设置中优于灵活接口执行变体；（4）简化策略在四分之三的设置中有效；（5）完整验证处理在所有设置中排名第一但资源消耗大；（6）gpt-5.6-sol验证变体完全解决所有公开游戏，达到约99% RHAE，总动作数不到人类基线的一半。

Conclusion: 组件贡献呈现非单调性：执行性世界模型的强制要求可能有害，简化策略在弱设置下无效，而验证机制是最关键的增强因素。最强模型组合验证策略实现了公开数据集饱和，但因模型晚于游戏发布，该结果仅代表公开集饱和而非泛化能力验证。

Abstract: Our previous ARC-AGI-3 agent bundled executable world modeling, scheduled simplification, and exact replay verification, leaving unclear which idea accounted for its performance. We address this attribution question with four nested Codex-based agents: a textual baseline; a flexible-interface executable world model without replay verification; the same executable model with scheduled simplification; and a fixed-interface verification treatment that retains simplification and requires exact reproduction of recorded observations. The main study evaluates all four agents with gpt-5.4 and gpt-5.5 at high and xhigh reasoning effort on the public ARC-AGI-3 games. Exploratory follow-ups evaluate the textual and verification variants with gpt-5.6-sol at xhigh and max. The most robust result is that every agent variant improves with a stronger model and with greater reasoning effort. Within each model-effort setting, differences among variants are smaller than anticipated, while the effects of individual components vary across settings. Requiring a persistent executable deliverable is not universally beneficial: the textual variant outperforms the flexible-interface executable variant in both gpt-5.5 settings. Simplification improves performance in three of the four model-effort settings, with the weakest setting as the only exception. The complete verification treatment ranks first in all four settings, although it uses substantially more resources. In the gpt-5.6-sol follow-up, the verification variant fully solves every public game at both reasoning efforts, achieves about 99% RHAE, and uses fewer than half the total actions of the human baseline. Because the model postdates these games and held-out performance remains untested, this result should be interpreted as saturation of the public set only.

</details>


### [16] [Beyond a Joke: Multi-Angle Reasoning for Detecting and Explaining Harmful Humor in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15442)
*Shanhong Liu,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh,Malika Meghjani,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MAR-12框架，通过12个理论视角和注意力机制实现网络迷因的幽默与仇恨检测，并生成可解释性推理，在准确率和解释质量上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络迷因融合视觉、文本和文化语境，幽默、讽刺与有害意图往往共存，使其理解极具挑战。现有可解释性需求迫切，但当前多模态分类器要么忽视这些相互依赖关系，要么仅提供有限的解释能力。

Method: 提出MAR-12框架：1）基于幽默与仇恨理论从12个结构化视角解读迷因；2）采用角色感知软门控注意力机制学习各视角的贡献权重；3）使用基于原型的分类器进行最终预测；4）结合视角推理与注意力权重合成透明且基于语境的解释。

Result: 在PrideMM和Memotion数据集上，幽默检测准确率达80.3%，仇恨检测准确率达75.9%，均超越现有最优方法。人类与GPT-4评估均证实MAR-12能生成连贯且有说服力的解释，尤其在幽默与有害线索共存时表现突出。

Conclusion: MAR-12有效整合理论驱动的多视角分析与注意力机制，在保持高分类性能的同时提供了可靠、透明的解释，为复杂迷因内容的理解与审核提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Internet memes intertwine visual cues, textual content, and cultural context, making them particularly challenging to interpret in scenarios where humor, sarcasm, and harmful intent coexist. These complexities highlight the need for explainable meme understanding systems that can provide reliable and structured reasoning to support both accurate classification and human interpretability. However, existing multimodal classifiers either overlook these interdependencies or provide only limited interpretability. In this paper, we introduce MAR-12, a novel framework that leverages Vision Language Models (VLMs) for meme detection and understanding in settings where humorous and hateful elements may coexist. The framework first interprets each meme through twelve structured perspectives derived from humor and hate theories. It then applies a role-aware soft-gated attention mechanism to learn how much each perspective should contribute, followed by a prototype-based classifier for the final prediction. Finally, explanations are synthesized using both perspective-specific reasoning and learned attention weights, ensuring transparent and context-grounded justifications. We evaluate MAR-12 on the PrideMM and Memotion datasets, where it achieves up to 80.3% accuracy for humor detection and 75.9% accuracy for hate detection, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. Furthermore, both human and GPT-4-based evaluations confirm that MAR-12 produces coherent and persuasive explanations, particularly for memes in which humorous and harmful cues co-occur.

</details>


### [17] [From Black Box to Executable Logic: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Prolog Expert Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15459)
*Eduardo C. Garrido-Merchán*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将训练好的深度强化学习策略转换为可解释Prolog逻辑程序的三阶段后处理方法，证明了在有限MDP中具有返回损失界限保证，在连续观测空间中可实现任意精度的转换（但代价随观测维度呈指数增长），并在多个任务上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练好的深度强化学习策略是一个黑箱，缺乏可解释性。研究者们希望将其重写为可执行的逻辑程序，使其既能复现策略行为，又能被人阅读、被逻辑引擎执行、被优化器编辑，从而实现策略的可解释性和可验证性。

Method: 提出三阶段后处理转换方法：(1)提取冻结的近端策略优化(PPO)教师模型；(2)采用经典关系学习方法从其决策中归纳有序规则列表；(3)输出为Prolog程序，由标准逻辑引擎执行每个决策。随后进行扩展阶段：编辑规则库，仅当策略评估认证返回值增加时才接受编辑。对于连续观测设置，采用命题阈值实例化方法，通过提高分辨率参数B来逼近网络决策边界。

Result: 在16,944个可达状态的房间任务中，扩展后的Prolog程序在所有种子上都达到精确最优返回，在预算受限设置下十次种子中有十次超越随机教师。在连续控制任务上，Acrobot任务用11个子句匹配神经教师（在噪声范围内），CartPole恢复约97%返回值，而LunarLander仅部分恢复——这与指数下界预测的性能上限完全吻合。理论分析证明：有限MDP中存在返回损失界限保证；连续观测空间中转换可实现任意精度（不一致性O(1/B)），但代价随观测维度呈指数增长。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将黑箱深度强化学习策略转换为可解释的逻辑程序，在理论上有严格保证（单调改进、有限终止、返回损失界限），在实践中表现良好。对于连续控制任务，观测维度是关键瓶颈：低维任务可高效转换，高维精细控制任务受指数代价限制只能部分恢复，这为实际应用提供了明确的能力边界和适用范围指导。

Abstract: A trained deep reinforcement learning policy is a black box, and we ask whether it can be made explainable by rewriting it as an executable logic program that reproduces its behaviour and that a person can read, a logic engine can run, and an optimizer can edit. We present a three-stage post-hoc transformation that extracts a frozen proximal policy optimization teacher, induces an ordered rule list from its decisions in the manner of classical relational learning, and emits the result as a Prolog program whose every decision is executed by an off-the-shelf logic engine; a subsequent expansion stage edits the rule base and accepts an edit only when policy evaluation certifies a return increase. We prove four guarantees. A return-loss bound makes the distilled program a machine-checkable certificate in a finite Markov decision process, and the expansion loop improves monotonically and terminates. For the continuous-observation setting we answer whether the conversion is possible at all: the propositional threshold instantiation converts the network to arbitrary fidelity as the resolution B grows, with disagreement O(1/B) and a return gap that closes at the same rate, and a matching lower bound shows the cost is exponential in the observation dimension for an oblique decision boundary. Empirically, on a two-room key-and-door task with 16,944 reachable states the expanded Prolog program attains exact optimal return in every seed and, in a budget-capped regime, exceeds the stochastic teacher on exact return in ten of ten seeds. On three continuous-control tasks the emitted program substitutes the network, matching the neural teacher within noise on Acrobot with eleven clauses and recovering about 97% of its return on CartPole, while on the finer-control LunarLander it recovers only partially, exactly the ceiling the exponential lower bound predicts.

</details>


### [18] [A Critical Analysis of Trustworthy AI Tools, Mark Frameworks, and the Implementation Chasms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15480)
*Michael Papademas,Xenia Ziouvelou,Kostas Karpouzis,Vangelis Karkaletsis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析OECD数据集，揭示了当前可信AI工具和认证框架在伦理覆盖范围、生命周期阶段和利益相关者参与方面存在显著不平衡，并提出了改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI伦理准则大量涌现，但这些框架普遍抽象且缺乏具体的实施机制，导致AI原则与实践之间持续存在鸿沟，需要系统地评估现有可信AI工具的实际效果和覆盖范围。

Method: 基于OECD的全面数据集，采用实证映射和描述性比较分析方法，对可信AI(TAI)的工具和信任标记框架进行系统性评估，分析其在伦理焦点、生命周期覆盖、利益相关者目标和工具类型学方面的特征。

Result: 发现现有工具严重偏向公平性、透明度和稳健性，而可解释性、数字安全和环境可持续性受到关注不足；大多数工具集中在开发后阶段，缺乏对早期设计和数据收集阶段的指导；教育倡议和政策参与明显发展不足，显示出当前TAI努力被行业内的技术和程序措施主导。

Conclusion: 弥合AI原则与实践之间的鸿沟需要扩展伦理目标、将伦理嵌入AI全生命周期、并促进更广泛的多利益相关者参与，以推进更全面、包容和可执行的AI治理。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems increasingly impact society, ensuring their ethical and trustworthy deployment has become a global priority. While a myriad of high-level ethical guidelines have emerged, criticism persists that these frameworks remain abstract and lack concrete mechanisms for implementation. This paper conducts a critical analysis of tools and trust mark frameworks intended to operationalize trustworthy AI (TAI), drawing on a comprehensive dataset from the OECD. Through empirical mapping and descriptive comparative analysis, we identify significant asymmetries in ethical focus, lifecycle coverage, stakeholder targeting, and tool typology. Our findings show a strong emphasis on fairness, transparency, and robustness, with comparatively little attention paid to explainability, digital security, and environmental sustainability. Moreover, most tools and certifications concentrate on post-development stages, with limited guidance for early design or data collection phases. Educational initiatives and policy engagement are notably underdeveloped, suggesting that current TAI efforts are dominated by technical and procedural measures within industry contexts. We argue that bridging the persistent chasm between AI principles and practice requires expanding ethical objectives, embedding ethics across the AI lifecycle, and fostering broader multi-stakeholder participation. This study provides both a diagnosis of existing implementation gaps and actionable recommendations for advancing more holistic, inclusive, and enforceable AI governance

</details>


### [19] [Logic, Optimization, and Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15532)
*J. N. Hooker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了逻辑与优化在基于规则的AI中的结合应用，探讨了如何通过这种结合实现AI的透明性、可解释性和可信性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI领域对透明性需求的日益增长（包括可重现性、可解释性、可信性和公平性），基于规则的AI提供了自然的透明性解决方案。逻辑作为编码规则库和推理的媒介，而优化作为计算推理的强大技术，两者的结合具有重要的现实意义。

Method: 文章调查了逻辑-优化合作的多个领域，包括概率逻辑、贝叶斯逻辑、信念逻辑与Dempster-Shafer理论、非单调（默认）逻辑、多值逻辑，以及基于布尔回归的噪声数据逻辑公式推断。同时探讨了使用决策图和逻辑型Benders分解来计算投影（逻辑和优化的基本问题），以及后最优分析在解释结论形成过程中的应用。

Result: 展示了如何通过逻辑与优化的结合实现基于规则的AI系统的透明性，包括使用决策图和逻辑型Benders分解计算投影，利用后最优分析解释结论的形成过程，以及在答案集编程模理论中优化方法的应用。

Conclusion: 逻辑与优化的结合为基于规则的AI提供了有效的透明性解决方案，不仅能够实现推理计算，还能解释推理过程，增强系统的可信度和可解释性。文章最后提出了未来可能的研究方向。

Abstract: Logic and optimization can, in combination, make valuable contributions to rule-based AI. Logic is the obvious medium for encoding a rule base and drawing inferences from it, while optimization provides a powerful technology for computing inferences. Their combination has taken on new relevance amid a growing concern for transparency in AI. which is important for reproducibility, explainability, trustworthiness, and fairness. Rule-based AI provides a natural solution to transparency that is becoming increasingly practical due to today's highly advanced optimization methods. This article surveys several areas of logic-optimization partnership, including probabilistic logic, Bayesian logic, belief logics and Dempster-Shafer theory, nonmonotonic (default) logic, many-valued logics, and inference of logical formulas from noisy data based on Boolean regression. It shows how to compute projections, the fundamental problem of both logic and optimization, using decision diagrams and logic-based Benders decomposition. It describes the use of postoptimality analysis to explain how conclusions are reached, further enhancing transparency, as well as the role of optimization in answer set programming modulo theories. The paper concludes by suggesting possible future research directions.

</details>


### [20] [SeerGuard: A Safety Framework for Mobile GUI Agents via World Model Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15550)
*Xue Yu,Bo Yuan,Pengshuai Yang,Kailin Zhao,Hong Hu,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SeerGuard，一种面向移动GUI代理的事前安全框架，通过指令级筛查和动作级风险评估，在不损失任务完成能力的前提下显著提升了系统安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI代理虽具备强大的自动化任务执行能力，但单次错误操作可能引发不可逆后果；现有安全机制多为被动响应式，无法在执行前预判风险，亟需能够提前评估后果的主动安全方案。

Method: 构建统一的安全增强世界模型(SAWM)，通过多任务学习将语义级下一状态预测与安全风险评估相结合，实现执行前的指令级筛查与动作级风险评估。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型上，当ω=0.8时安全-效用分数从0.191提升至0.596，当α=0.8时风险代价分数从0.347降低至0.130；验证了框架在多样化移动GUI代理上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SeerGuard通过事前的多层级风险评估机制，有效降低了移动GUI代理的安全风险，同时保持了任务执行效用，为构建更安全的自动化系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Mobile graphical user interface (GUI) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in automating complex tasks, yet they introduce critical safety risks where a single erroneous action can lead to irreversible consequences. Existing safety mechanisms are primarily reactive, lacking the ability to assess risks before execution. In this paper, we introduce SeerGuard, a consequence-aware safety framework designed to mitigate these risks through pre-execution instruction-level screening and action-level risk assessment. Specifically, the action-level assessment analyzes agent-proposed actions within current GUI states, anticipating likely outcomes to identify risks before they are executed. To enable these capabilities, we construct a unified safety-augmented world model (SAWM) via multi-task learning, integrating semantic next-state prediction with safety risk assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SeerGuard generalizes effectively across diverse mobile GUI agents. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, it increases the safety-utility score from $0.191$ to $0.596$ at $ω=0.8$ and reduces the risk-cost score from $0.347$ to $0.130$ at $α=0.8$. Further analyses on our SAWM validate the effectiveness of the instruction-level screening, alongside the capability of action risk assessment and next-state prediction.

</details>


### [21] [MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15592)
*Xu Hou,Meiyu Liang,Wei Huang,Yawen Li,Zhe Xue,Wu Liu,Guanhua Ye,Lei Shi,Kangkang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MGDT框架，采用先对齐后扩散范式解决多模态知识图谱补全问题，通过RASR-MoE模块选择关系相关的多模态语义路径，利用冻结MLLM对齐表示，再通过KGDT进行图条件去噪生成，在三个基准数据集上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的多模态知识图谱补全方法直接对原始多模态特征去噪，迫使去噪器同时执行关系依赖的线索选择、跨模态语义对齐和结构感知实体生成，为扩散引入了噪声和语义不一致的条件，导致补全性能次优。

Method: 提出MGDT框架，采用先对齐后扩散范式。首先使用关系自适应语义路由混合专家（RASR-MoE）模块选择关系相关的多模态语义转换路径并抑制无关模态干扰；然后使用冻结的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为语义锚点，将路由后的多模态表示对齐到统一潜在空间；最后知识图谱扩散Transformer（KGDT）在对齐空间中执行图条件去噪生成以产生缺失实体表示。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，MGDT始终优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: MGDT通过解耦多模态语义对齐与扩散生成过程，有效缓解了现有方法中条件噪声和语义不一致问题，为多模态知识图谱补全任务提供了一种新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion (MKGC) requires inferring missing entities from structural, textual, and visual cues. Existing diffusion-based MKGC methods usually denoise directly on raw multimodal features. Such a design forces the denoiser to simultaneously perform relation-dependent cue selection, cross-modal semantic alignment, and structure-aware entity generation, which introduces noisy and semantically inconsistent conditions for diffusion and consequently leads to suboptimal completion performance. To address this limitation, we propose MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts (MGDT), a novel MKGC framework built on an align-then-diffuse paradigm. MGDT first employs a Relation-Adaptive Semantic Routing Mixture-of-Experts (RASR-MoE) module to select relation-relevant multimodal semantic transformation paths and suppress irrelevant modality interference. MGDT then uses a frozen Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a semantic anchor to align the routed multimodal representations into a unified latent space and reduce cross-modal semantic heterogeneity. Finally, a Knowledge Graph Diffusion Transformer (KGDT) performs graph-conditioned denoising generation in the aligned space to produce the missing entity representation. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that MGDT consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [22] [Neuro-Symbolic AI for LEED compliance: Document-Centric Benchmarking, Deterministic Numeric Checking, and When Multimodal Hurts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15647)
*Aritro De,Juliana Felkner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号流水线，用于LEED v4.1 BD+C认证文档的自动化合规性筛选。在四个大学建筑项目（484个PDF，153个信用级决策）上，4B参数的本地模型（gemma3:4b）实现了67.3%的准确率，超越了8B参数模型。确定性数值检查器成功修正了关键定量信用的算术错误（EA-p2准确率从50%提升至100%），但完整的神经符号配置总体准确率为61.6%，落后于最佳纯文本基线。研究发现低分辨率图纸图像会降低准确性，且提示策略的有效性取决于项目的文档丰富程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEED v4.1 BD+C认证过程高度依赖文档审阅，审核人员需要阅读数百页的项目证据材料并手工应用特定信用的阈值逻辑，这一过程耗时费力、成本高昂。面对这一挑战，论文旨在探索小型、本地部署的语言模型能否对LEED文档执行有效的筛选工作，以及确定性的符号组件应如何与神经模型分工协作。

Method: 论文提出了一种神经符号流水线，包含以下核心组件：(1) 将项目PDF对齐到LEED信用部分的文档对齐模块；(2) 基于信用感知关键词签名的证据检索系统；(3) 使用本地部署的4B参数语言模型（gemma3:4b）进行合规性验证；(4) 应用于定量阈值的LEED特定数值检查器。研究在四个大学建筑项目上进行了系统实验和消融研究。

Result: 实验结果显示：4B参数的gemma3:4b模型作为纯文本核心验证器表现最佳，达到67.3%准确率并超越更大的8B参数llama3.1:8b模型。确定性数值检查器有效修正了关键定量信用的算术错误，将EA-p2准确率从50%提升至100%。然而，完整神经符号配置总体准确率为61.6%，落后于最佳纯文本基线，主要归因于提取失败和对定性类别的保守行为。消融研究表明：添加低分辨率图纸图像（150-300 dpi）会一致性地降低准确性；提示策略有效性取决于项目的PASS率——规则型提示在文档丰富项目中表现最佳，而链式思考提示在文档稀缺项目中更优。

Conclusion: 该研究在LEED v4.1 BD+C合规性验证这一特定领域内，建立了一个可复现的基线参考点，明确了当前方法的准确性水平和失败模式。研究揭示了小型本地模型在这一任务中的潜力与局限，特别是符号组件与神经模型的有效分工策略，为后续研究提供了重要参考。同时也指出了当前方法的主要瓶颈：提取失败和对定性类别处理过于保守。

Abstract: LEED v4.1 BD+C certification remains a document-intensive process that requires reviewers to read hundreds of pages of project evidence and apply credit-specific threshold logic by hand. This paper investigates whether small, locally deployed language models can perform meaningful screening of LEED documentation and how deterministic symbolic components should share that work. A neuro-symbolic pipeline is introduced that aligns project PDFs to LEED credit sections, retrieves evidence with credit-aware keyword signatures, verifies compliance with a locally hosted 4-billion-parameter language model, and applies a LEED-specific numeric checker to quantitative thresholds. Experiments on four university buildings (484 PDFs, 153 credit-level decisions) show that a 4-billion-parameter model (gemma3:4b) is the strongest text-only core verifier, achieving 67.3% accuracy and outperforming a larger 8-billion-parameter model (llama3.1:8b) in this task. The deterministic numeric checker corrects arithmetic errors on key quantitative credits, moving EA-p2 from 50% to 100% accuracy and improving several other credits when required values are reliably extracted. At the same time, the full neuro-symbolic configuration achieves 61.6% overall accuracy, trailing the best text-only baseline due to extraction failures and conservative behavior on qualitative categories. Systematic ablations show that adding low-resolution drawing images (150-300 dpi) consistently reduces accuracy, and that prompt effectiveness depends on the building's ground-truth PASS rate: rubric prompts perform best on documentation-rich projects, while chain-of-thought prompts perform best on documentation-lean projects. Within the specific scope of LEED v4.1 BD+C compliance verification over raw project documentation, this pipeline and its baselines provide an initial reproducible reference point for both accuracy and failure modes.

</details>


### [23] [ToolVerse: Unlocking Massive Environments and Long-Horizon Tasks for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15660)
*Shuaiyu Zhou,Fengpeng Yue,Zengjie Hu,Yuanzhe Shen,Chenyang Zhang,feng hong,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ToolVerse框架，通过大规模MCP环境构建、基于工具依赖图的长程任务生成以及细粒度优势算法，显著增强了LLM在动态真实环境中的长程工具使用与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在紧凑、定义良好的场景中表现出较强的推理能力，但在需要无缝工具集成的大规模、多样、动态的真实世界环境中，难以保持鲁棒性和有效性。

Method: 1) 基于近400个真实世界MCP（约4500个工具）自动构建大规模可执行智能体训练环境；2) 提出基于工具依赖图的任务设计策略，采用动态解锁采样算法生成GUST数据集；3) 提出细粒度的Turn-Aware Relative Advantage算法以缓解长程智能体RL中的信用分配问题。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上的实验表明，该方法显著增强了LLM在长程工具使用方面的能力，性能明显提升，并在动态环境中展现出稳健的推理能力。

Conclusion: ToolVerse框架有效提升了LLM智能体在复杂动态环境中的长程推理与工具集成能力，为智能体RL训练提供了可扩展的环境与有效的算法支持。

Abstract: While LLM agents demonstrate strong reasoning abilities in compact and well-defined scenarios, they struggle to maintain robustness and effectiveness when faced with large-scale, diverse, and dynamic real-world environments that demand seamless tool integration. To address this gap, we introduce ToolVerse, a comprehensive framework that scales up agentic RL environments and enables agents to perform complex long-horizon reasoning in Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) tasks. First, ToolVerse automatically builds the massive executable agent training environments from nearly 400 real-world Model Context Protocols (MCPs) that contain about 4500 tools. Second, we propose a task design strategy based on a tool dependency graph, utilizing Dynamic Unlocking Sampling Algorithm to generate long-horizon tasks, and produce GUST (Graph Unlocking Sampling Tasks) dataset. Third, to alleviate the credit assigment problem in long-horizon agentic RL, we propose a fine-grained Turn-Aware Relative Advantage algorithm. We conduct extensive Agentic RL training using ToolVerse and evaluate our framework on serveral agentic benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly strengthens LLMs' capabilities in long-horizon tool use, achieving a marked performance boost and showcasing robust reasoning within dynamic environments.

</details>


### [24] [S1-Omni: A Unified Multimodal Reasoning Model for Scientific Understanding, Prediction, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15686)
*Jiahao Zhao,Junyi Liu,Lifeng Xu,Nan Xu,Qingli Wang,Qingxiao Li,Tianle Chen,Xiaoyu Wu,Yawen Zheng,Zikai Wang,Guanming Liu,Hequn Zhou,Jingyi Wang,Jingyuan Shu,Keqi Wang,Li He,Songyang Diao,Wenhui Xu,Xinyu Ren,Yaqin Fan,Yujin Zhou,Zhanao Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出S1-Omni，一个统一的多模态科学推理模型，通过整合科学数据表示、知识对齐和任务解码，在60+基准测试中超越GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro，实现了科学理解、预测和生成的统一建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI for Science领域存在模型能力高度碎片化的问题，领域特定模型、工具增强的LLMs和科学语言模型各自为政，难以联合建模异构数据、科学规律和专家知识，这限制了科学AI的整体能力发展。

Method: S1-Omni采用三层架构：1）统一科学数据表示，将自然语言指令和CIF、SMILES、蛋白质序列、光谱、科学图像等科学对象映射到共享表示空间；2）自然世界知识对齐，在数据构建和训练中融入科学规律和专家知识；3）领域特定任务解码，支持性质预测、光谱到分子生成、蛋白质位点和结构预测、科学图像生成编辑等任务。模型在包含200个科学任务、数百万推理样本的S1-Omni-Corpus上训练。

Result: 在60+科学基准测试上评估，S1-Omni在大多数基准上超越GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro，在多个基准上匹配或超越领域特定模型，展现出强大的统一科学建模能力。

Conclusion: S1-Omni为统一科学建模提供了一条可行的实践路径，通过单一连贯的科学推理模型整合了原本碎片化的模型能力，证明了统一多模态科学推理的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: We present S1-Omni, a unified multimodal reasoning model for scientific understanding, prediction, and generation. AI for Science (AI4S) has advanced significantly through domain-specific models, tool-augmented LLMs, and scientific language models. However, model capabilities remain highly fragmented, limiting the joint modeling of heterogeneous data, scientific laws, and expert knowledge. S1-Omni addresses this gap by consolidating these capabilities into a single, coherent scientific reasoning model. The architecture of S1-Omni is built upon three core components: unified representation of scientific data, natural-world knowledge alignment, and decoding for domain-specific tasks. First, S1-Omni maps natural-language instructions and scientific objects, including CIF, SMILES, protein sequences, spectra, and scientific images, into a shared representation space. Second, it incorporates scientific laws and expert knowledge into data construction and training, enabling the model to reason from scientific evidence. Third, it performs task-specific decoding to support a broad range of applications, including property prediction, spectrum-to-molecular generation, protein site and structure prediction, and scientific image generation and editing. S1-Omni is trained on S1-Omni-Corpus, which covers 200 scientific tasks and contains millions of reasoning samples, and is evaluated on over 60 scientific benchmarks. It outperforms GPT-5.5 and Gemini-3.1-Pro on most benchmarks and matches or surpasses domain-specific models on several benchmarks. Overall, S1-Omni provides a practical path toward unified scientific modeling.

</details>


### [25] [Behavioral Controllability of Agentic Models for Information Extraction: From Fixed Workflows to Reflective Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15715)
*Lujia Zhang,Xingzhou Chen,Hongwei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过会议论文数据集提取任务，系统比较了固定流程基线与反射型智能体变体的性能差异，重点分析反思和记忆等智能体组件是否带来可观测且可控的改进，并提出了优化的智能体设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在复杂信息抽取任务中的应用日益增加，但尚不清楚反思和记忆等智能体组件是否比固定的LLM工作流程带来可观测且可控的改进。这一问题的澄清对于智能体系统的设计与应用具有重要意义。

Method: 研究者采用会议论文数据集提取作为基准任务，对比了固定工作流程基线与反射型智能体变体的性能。研究重点关注过程级行为（包括工具执行、重试、反思、记忆使用、运行时和失败恢复），同时将抽取覆盖率和字段完整性作为次要结果指标。研究还设计了一个优化的智能体条件（S2），引入了更丰富的PDF工具和动态工具选择机制。

Result: 研究刻画了智能体机制何时改变系统行为、这些变化是否改善任务完成度，以及观察到的失败模式如何激励在相同评估框架下的优化智能体设计。分析揭示了反思和记忆等组件的具体作用机制及其对系统性能的影响方式。

Conclusion: 智能体机制确实能够改变系统行为，但其对任务完成度的改善效果取决于具体实现方式。通过分析失败模式可以指导优化智能体设计，从而在保持评估一致性的前提下提升系统性能。该研究为智能体组件的设计提供了实证依据和分析框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for complex information-extraction tasks, yet it remains unclear whether agentic components such as reflection and memory lead to observable and controllable improvements over fixed LLM workflows. We study this question through conference-paper dataset extraction, where a system must identify datasets mentioned in scholarly PDFs and produce structured records. We compare a fixed workflow baseline with reflective agent variants and specify an optimized agent condition (S2) that extends the same task with richer PDF tools and dynamic tool selection. Our evaluation emphasizes process-level behavior--including tool execution, retries, reflection, memory use, runtime, and failure recovery--while treating extraction coverage and field completeness as secondary outcome measures. The paper characterizes when agentic mechanisms change system behavior, whether these changes improve task completion, and how the observed failure modes motivate an optimized agent design under the same evaluation harness.

</details>


### [26] [NeurOWL: An LLM-Based Neural-symbolic Framework for Incomplete OWL Ontology Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15776)
*Hui Yang,Jiaoyan Chen,Yiping Song,Renate Schmidt,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeurOWL框架，用于解决不完整本体中的子类推理问题，能够同时进行子类关系验证和本体溯因推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的本体通常是不完整的，这给语义推理带来了挑战。现有的子类推理在面对不完整本体时存在局限性，而传统的本体溯因推理需要预定义的候选缺失公理集合，限制了其实用性。

Method: 提出NeurOWL，一个端到端的神经符号框架，结合形式化定义的语义和文本语义。该框架利用大语言模型和本体嵌入技术，统一了子类验证与本体溯因推理，并移除了对预定义候选缺失公理集合的需求。

Result: 在多个领域的真实本体上进行了评估，NeurOWL在不同领域均展现出强大且鲁棒的性能。

Conclusion: NeurOWL成功解决了不完整本体中的子类推理问题，能够判断子类关系的语义合理性并提供包含潜在缺失公理的逻辑解释，为本体补全和推理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: OWL ontologies provide a formal knowledge representation framework that enables semantic reasoning, and have been widely adopted across domains such as healthcare and bioinformatics. In practice, however, real-world ontologies are often incomplete, which pose challenges for reasoning. In this work, we focus on a fundamental subsumption reasoning problem: given an incomplete ontology and a candidate (non-entailed) subsumption, determine whether the subsumption is semantically plausible and, if so, providing a logically sound explanation containing potential missing axioms. This task unifies subsumption verification with ontology abduction, and generalizes the latter by removing the need for a predefined candidate set of missing axioms. To address this subsumption reasoning problem, we propose NeurOWL, an end-to-end neuro-symbolic framework that jointly performs verification and abduction, leveraging both formally defined semantics and textual semantics through Large Language Models and ontology embeddings. We evaluate NeurOWL on real-world ontologies across multiple domains, demonstrating strong and robust performance across different domains.

</details>


### [27] [Knowledge-Centric Agents for Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15845)
*Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Ruibo Ming,He Zhang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以知识为中心的框架，通过知识反演、注入和推理来解决可视化创作系统（如ComfyUI）中的工作流生成问题，显著提升了生成质量、结构连贯性和执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型方法将工作流生成视为简单的文本到JSON生成任务，存在结构脆弱性，缺乏专家级的模块组合推理能力和设计所需的经验知识。成功的工作流生成需要建模知识本身，包括其结构、层次和推理动态。

Method: 提出一个以知识为中心的框架：1) 知识反演：从大量真实工作流中提炼层次化表示（从伪代码、骨架到高层策略）；2) 知识注入：通过监督微调，教会模型从任务描述推理到策略，再从策略推理到可执行结构；3) 可逆推理：合成可执行工作流，并通过自精炼增强结构连贯性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法生成的工作流具有更丰富的节点多样性、更连贯的结构和更高的执行成功率，超越了现有系统的表现。

Conclusion: 为知识驱动的智能体工作流生成奠定了新的基础，证明了以知识为中心的方法在复杂系统设计中的有效性。

Abstract: Workflow generation in visual creation systems such as ComfyUI demands not only syntactic accuracy but also expert-level reasoning over modular compositions. Existing large language model (LLM) approaches often treat this as a direct text-to-JSON generation task, struggling with structural brittleness and lacking the experiential knowledge required for effective design. We argue that successful workflow generation requires modeling knowledge itself, including its structure, hierarchy, and reasoning dynamics. To this end, we propose a knowledge-centric framework that learns to invert, inject, and infer with knowledge across multiple abstraction levels. We first perform knowledge inversion to distill hierarchical representations, ranging from full pseudo-codes and skeletons to high-level strategies, from large collections of real-world workflows. We then conduct knowledge injection through supervised fine-tuning, teaching the model to reason from task descriptions to strategies and from strategies to executable structures. During inference, the model performs reversible reasoning to synthesize executable workflows, augmented by self-refinement for structural coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces workflows with richer node diversity, more coherent structures, and higher execution success rates than existing systems, establishing a new foundation for knowledge-driven, agentic workflow generation.

</details>


### [28] [DSWorld: A Data Science World Model for Efficient Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15901)
*Zherui Yang,Fan Liu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了数据科学世界模型概念，通过预测操作效果来避免昂贵的试错计算，实现了14倍训练加速和3-6倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主数据科学代理严重依赖试错工作流程，导致计算成本高昂。核心瓶颈在于缺乏能够在真实执行前预测数据科学操作效果的模型。

Method: 提出Data Science World Model概念，构建DSWorld框架，包含结构化状态构建、成本感知路由、轻量级真实执行和基于LLM的模拟器。构建8K规模的轨迹转换数据集，引入Reflective World Model Optimization（一种错误感知的强化学习策略）来改进转换预测。

Result: DSWorld在RL代理训练上实现约14倍加速，在搜索推理上实现约3-6倍加速，同时保持竞争力性能。在转换预测任务上比最强LLM基线提升35.6%。

Conclusion: 通过引入世界模型来预测数据科学操作的执行效果，可以显著减少试错成本并提升效率，为自主数据科学代理提供了新的发展方向。

Abstract: Despite strong capabilities in data understanding and decision-making, autonomous data science agents still heavily rely on trial-and-error workflows that involve expensive computation. This bottleneck motivates models that can anticipate the effects of data science operations before real execution. In this paper, we introduce the concept of Data Science World Model, which model the data science execution environment by predicting environment state transitions conditioned on current workflow states and candidate operations. We further propose DSWorld, a practical framework that combines structured state construction, cost-aware routing, lightweight real execution, and an LLM-based simulator for expensive operations. To support training, we construct an 8K-scale transition trajectory dataset and introduce Reflective World Model Optimization, an error-aware reinforcement learning strategy for improving transition prediction. Experiments show that DSWorld accelerates RL-based agent training by approximately $14\times$ and search-based inference by approximately $3$-$6\times$ while maintaining competitive performance, and outperforms the strongest LLM baseline by 35.6% on transition prediction tasks. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DSWorld.

</details>


### [29] [A Formally Grounded ODRL Evaluator: Implementation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15987)
*Jaime Osvaldo Salas,Paolo Pareti,Adeel Aslam,Christopher Maidens,George Konstantinidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为ODRL策略语言提出了首个具有透明形式语义的评估器，解决了当前标准缺乏数学形式语义导致的互操作性和一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODRL策略语言正成为欧洲数据空间中策略建模的事实标准，但当前标准缺乏数学形式语义来描述系统应如何实现策略评估。这导致各种系统和工具实现了各自的语言解释，限制了互操作性且无法保证一致结果。

Method: 基于现有的ODRL语义模型，形式化定义了静态和流式设置下的访问控制和监控场景的ODRL评估问题，并提供了新颖高效的算法和实现。开发了首个支持所有规则类型且具有透明形式语义的ODRL评估器。

Result: 通过实验测量了系统性能，分析了与策略复杂性和策略评估数据规模相关的不同可扩展性维度。与现有最先进系统进行了比较，提供了现有ODRL评估器的比较综述，突出了支持的ODRL特性和评估模式的差异。

Conclusion: 提出了首个具有透明形式语义并支持所有规则类型的ODRL评估器，为解决ODRL策略评估的互操作性和一致性问题提供了坚实的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: The ODRL policy language is emerging as the de-facto standard for policy modelling data access and usage preferences, AI governance policies and data workflows in European dataspaces. The current standard has no mathematical formal semantics to describe how a system should implement policy evaluation. This has resulted in a variety of systems and tools that implement their own interpretation of the language, which limits interoperability and cannot guarantee consistent results. Based on an existing semantic model of ODRL, we formalise the problems of ODRL evaluation for the access control and monitoring scenarios, in both static and streaming settings, and we provide a novel, efficient algorithm and implementation. We present the first ODRL Evaluator with transparent formal semantics and supporting all rule types. We experimentally measure its performance, analysing different scalability dimensions related to policy complexity and size of the data on which a policy is evaluated. We compare our system with the state-of-the-art by providing a comparative review of existing ODRL evaluators, which highlights the differences in supported ODRL features and evaluation modes.

</details>


### [30] [Closing the AI Trust Gap: The Case for Independent Certification for Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15992)
*Trisevgeni Papakonstantinou,Cansu Canca,Farah Nanji,Waheedullah Pardess,Jen Weedon,Jasmijn Remmers,Eliza Krigman,Matthew Ball,Yalda Daryani,Kiran Iqbal,Francielle Vargas,María Llorente Sánchez,Joe Humphreys,Fendi Tsim,Kelly Fitzpatrick,Jeff Dunn,Catherine Feldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出负责任AI领域存在“信任鸿沟”问题——组织的内部治理努力无法转化为外部可验证的信任信号。作者提出应从关注内部流程转向关注可验证的结果，通过独立的、以结果为导向的认证机制来弥合这一鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管过去十年负责任AI（RAI）实践取得了大量进展，但市场上仍缺乏对值得信赖的AI系统的有效认可和奖励机制。认真投资于安全、公平和监督的企业无法向消费者、监管者和股东证明其系统超越了最低合规标准，导致“信任鸿沟”：负责任的开发努力发生在组织内部，却无法产生外部独立的、可验证的信任信号。

Method: 本文通过回顾现有的AI治理工具，并将其与医疗、可持续性和安全领域的认证制度进行比较分析，识别出当前治理框架的不足。同时，作者分析了导致信任鸿沟持续存在的三个复合性失败：市场无法区分可信系统与其仿制品；评估针对模型和输出而非部署的社会技术系统及其结果；测量生态系统倾向于避免危害而非展示益处。

Result: 研究发现现有的AI治理工具均未能将治理基线、独立验证的积极结果证据和市场信号整合在单一框架中。当前评估体系存在结构性缺陷，无法为社会提供识别和比较AI系统可信度的有效机制。

Conclusion: 本文提出独立的、以结果为导向的认证作为连接层，能够弥合信任鸿沟。这种认证机制通过使可信度变得可测量、可比较和具有商业回报价值，来补充监管和内部治理，从而建立一个能够奖励真正值得信赖的AI系统的市场环境。

Abstract: Over the past decade, responsible AI (RAI) has produced a substantial body of practice for identifying and mitigating the risks AI poses in high-stakes settings. Yet this work has not produced a market that rewards trustworthiness. Firms that invest seriously in safety, fairness, and oversight cannot consistently prove to consumers, regulators, and shareholders that their systems go beyond the bare minimum of compliance. What is missing is a way for society to recognize or compare the difference. The result is a trust gap: a structural condition in which responsible development efforts happen inside organizations but produce no external, independently recognized and verifiable signal of trustworthy outcomes. We argue this gap is sustained in part because of a focus on responsible AI (a matter of internal process) as opposed to trustworthy AI (a matter of independently verifiable real-world outcomes), and that it persists because of three compounding failures: (1) the market cannot distinguish trustworthy systems from their imitations; (2) evaluation targets models and outputs rather than deployed sociotechnical systems and their outcomes; (3) the measurement ecosystem is oriented toward avoiding harm rather than demonstrating benefit. Reviewing existing AI governance instruments and comparing them to certification regimes in healthcare, sustainability, and security, we show that none integrate a governance baseline, independently verified positive-outcome evidence, and market signaling in a single framework. We propose independent, outcome-oriented certification as the connective layer that can close the trust gap, complementing regulation and internal governance by making trustworthiness measurable, comparable, and commercially rewarded.

</details>


### [31] [SciForge: An AI-Native, Multimodal Workbench for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16038)
*SciForge Team,Zhangyang Gao,Minghao Fang,Yifei Liu,Hanhui Yang,Xinyu Gu,Shixiang Tang,Siqi Sun,Lei Bai,Cheng Tan,Mengdi Liu,Hao Wu,Shuizhou Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciForge是一个面向科学研究的原生AI工作台，通过五大核心支柱解决科研工作中异构对象的连贯性和可审计性问题，实现目标导向、多模态、可审计、协作式的科学研究流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学工作涉及论文、代码、数据集、文件格式、模型输出、图表、手稿和团队决策等异构对象，但现有通用AI助手无法将这些对象保持为连贯、可审计的研究状态，缺乏对复杂科研流程的有效支持。

Method: 提出SciForge系统，采用五大支柱设计：目标导向的科学决策治理、翻译后推理的多模态处理、证据治理的可审计追踪、协作式团队科学、实际应用场景。系统架构包括交互层、研究能力模式、Agent运行时和工作流引擎、证据DAG审计副驾和科学模型路由器，将GUI保留给人类判断，其他功能作为模块化服务运行。

Result: 通过八个端到端用户案例验证系统有效性，包括基因发现的多日代理研究冲刺、AI引导的从头蛋白质设计、分子优化和基因组到BGC发现等旗舰演示，系统目前作为桌面应用运行并支持移动端监督，已开源发布。

Conclusion: SciForge成功构建了一个多模态研究原生AI工作台，实现了科学对象的连贯管理和可审计追踪，为科研工作者提供了实用的AI辅助工具，未来版本将进一步深化团队协作功能。

Abstract: Scientific work increasingly spans heterogeneous artifacts -- papers, code, datasets, scientific file formats, model outputs, figures, manuscripts, and team decisions -- yet general-purpose AI assistants rarely preserve these objects as a coherent, auditable research state. We present SciForge, a multimodal research-native AI workbench that reserves the graphical interface for human judgment while search, parsing, model routing, workflow execution, plotting, writing, and presentation generation run as modular agent-accessible services. SciForge is built around five pillars: (i) \emph{goal-scoped scientific decision governance} for \textbf{goal-oriented} research, with review gates and shared review surfaces; (ii) \emph{translate-then-reason} for \textbf{multimodal} input, routing scientific objects through domain translators before the agent reasons; (iii) \emph{evidence governance} for \textbf{auditable} traceability, linking claims to provenance chains and audit findings; (iv) \emph{collaborative team science} for \textbf{collaborative} research, enabling multi-role decision governance, with shared team workspaces planned for future releases; and (v) \emph{real-world application scenarios} for \textbf{practical} impact, demonstrated through eight end-to-end user cases, with flagship demonstrations including multi-day agentic research sprints for gene discovery, AI-guided de novo protein design, molecular optimization, and genome-to-BGC discovery. The system combines a thin interaction layer, contextual research capability patterns, an Agent Runtime and Workflow Engine, an Evidence-DAG audit sidecar and a Scientific Model Router. SciForge currently runs as a desktop application, with mobile supervision support; future releases will deepen team collaboration. The system is open-source and available at https://github.com/AGI4Sci/SciForge

</details>


### [32] [Harmonizing AI Safety Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16112)
*Wilber Sean Anterola,Matthew Ball,Luis F. Lafuerza,Markov Grey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对前沿AI公司能力阈值不统一的问题，提出了一种在三个风险领域（网络/生物滥用和AI研发自动化）构建协调阈值的方法论，以解决第三方验证困难和安全标准可能出现的逐底竞争问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI公司发布的能力阈值差异巨大，导致第三方难以验证阈值是否被突破或跨公司比较要求；缺乏共同最低阈值可能导致风险缓解不一致，造成安全标准的逐底竞争。

Method: 针对三个风险领域开发协调阈值方法：对于滥用风险（网络和生物），以预期危害为关键原语，采用显式风险建模方法，考虑风险渠道和模型发布条件；对于自动化AI研发，基于观察到的AI进展速率而非预期危害来设定阈值。

Result: 扩展了先前工作，揭示了现有实证差距和局限性，为不同风险领域提供了具体的风险建模方法和阈值设定依据。

Conclusion: 提出了跨风险领域协调阈值的系统性方法论，为建立统一的AI安全标准提供了理论基础，同时指出了当前研究存在的实证空白需要进一步填补。

Abstract: Frontier AI companies have published capability thresholds that differ substantially, making it difficult for third parties to verify whether a threshold has been crossed or to compare requirements across companies. Moreover, without common minimum thresholds, risk mitigation may be inconsistent, creating a potential race to the bottom in safety standards. We develop a methodology for deriving harmonized thresholds across three risk domains. For misuse risks (cyber and biological), we take expected harm as the key primitive and use an explicit risk-modeling approach that accounts for risk channels and model release conditions. For automated AI R&D, we base our proposed threshold on the observed rate of AI progress rather than expected harm. Our analysis expands upon prior work and highlights existing empirical gaps and limitations.

</details>


### [33] [CRAFT: Clustering Rubrics to Diagnose Weak LLM Capabilities and Generate Targeted Fine-Tuning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16122)
*Vipul Gupta,Zihao Wang,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,MohammadHossein Rezaei,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRAFT方法，将基于评分标准的评估数据集转换为模型特定的能力诊断，通过识别具体的能力缺陷来指导目标性监督微调数据的生成，从而提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估流程只能识别模型在哪些样本、主题或类别上失败，但无法解释失败的根本原因（即能力缺陷）。这种“知其然而不知其所以然”的诊断方式无法有效指导下一轮模型迭代和目标性训练数据生成。因此需要一种能够从能力层面诊断模型弱点的方法。

Method: CRAFT将每个评分标准视为能力探针：从每个提示-评分标准对中提取能力描述，将这些描述聚类为分层能力树，在树的每个节点对目标模型进行评分，并动态选择不同树层级中表现不佳的节点。选定的弱能力节点随后指导目标性监督微调数据的生成。方法在评分标准层面而非提示或类别层面诊断弱点。

Result: 在四个开源模型、两个专业领域（金融和法律）以及13个与诊断数据不相交的保留基准测试上，CRAFT相比提示级EvalTree聚类和非目标性随机生成表现最佳。在金融领域，CRAFT在所有四个模型上均达到最强平均性能；在法律领域，四个模型中有三个表现最强，第四个也在最佳基线的解码方差范围内。

Conclusion: 在评分标准层面诊断弱点（而非提示或类别层面）既能更清晰地呈现模型能力缺陷，又能通过基于该诊断的微调产生可测量的模型改进，证明了细粒度能力诊断对模型迭代优化的价值。

Abstract: Evaluations should do more than measure a models current performance. They should tell us what to fix for the next model iteration and provide a way to generate targeted post training data. Most evaluation pipelines identify weak examples, topics, or categories, but they leave the underlying capability failure implicit: they say where a model fails, not why. We introduce CRAFT, a method that converts any rubric based evaluation dataset into a model specific diagnosis of weak capabilities. CRAFT treats each grading criterion as a capability probe: it extracts a capability description from every prompt rubric pair, clusters these descriptions into a hierarchical capability tree, scores the target model at every node, and selects low performing nodes dynamically across tree levels, at the granularity where each failure is clearest. The selected weak capabilities then direct the generation of targeted supervised finetuning data. Holding the data generation, finetuning, and evaluation setup fixed, we compare CRAFT against prompt level EvalTree clustering and untargeted random generation on four open source models, two professional domains (finance and legal), and 13 held out benchmarks disjoint from the diagnostic data. CRAFT achieves the strongest finance domain average for all four models under repeated temperature decoding; on legal domain, it is strongest for three of four models and remains within the decoding variance bands of the best baseline on the fourth. Diagnosing weaknesses at the level of rubric criteria, rather than prompts or categories, thus yields both a sharper picture of what a model cannot do and measurably better models after finetuning on that diagnosis.

</details>


### [34] [Beyond a Single Direction: Chain-of-Thought Disrupts Simple Steering of Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26772)
*Kia-Jüng Yang,Dominik Meier,Jiachen Zhao,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现大型推理模型(LRMs)的拒绝机制由残差流激活和思维链(CoT)共同编码。激活引导仅在39%的情况下逆转拒绝，但移除CoT后提升至70%，两阶段干预可达94%。CoT可作为独立载体重构合规信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型生成思维链痕迹后产生最终输出，引入动态内部状态使得拒绝等控制机制变得复杂。与仅由单一方向子空间中介拒绝的指令微调LLMs不同，LRMs中的拒绝机制额外依赖于思维链(CoT)，这种差异促使研究者探索CoT对拒绝机制的具体影响。

Method: 使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B模型进行实验：(1)保持CoT固定条件下进行激活引导，观察拒绝逆转率；(2)完全移除CoT后进行激活引导；(3)设计两阶段干预，让模型在激活引导下重新生成CoT；(4)分析移除引导后CoT单独保留的效果。

Result: 保持CoT固定时激活引导仅逆转39%的拒绝案例，完全移除CoT后提升至70%。两阶段干预（模型在激活引导下重新生成CoT）实现94%的拒绝逆转率。即使移除引导，结果CoT单独仍保留48%的效果。这表明CoT能够独立携带并重构合规信号。

Conclusion: LRMs中的拒绝机制由残差流激活和思维链(CoT)联合编码。这种联合编码使LRMs对纯激活级干预更具鲁棒性，但同时也暴露了CoT作为替代攻击面的可能性。这一发现对理解和干预LRMs的安全机制具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before producing final outputs, introducing a dynamic internal state that may complicate control mechanisms such as refusal. Unlike instruction-tuned LLMs, where refusal is mediated by a single directional subspace, refusal in large reasoning models (LRMs) additionally depends on the CoT. In DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B, activation steering reverses refusal in only 39% of cases when the CoT is kept fixed, but removing the CoT entirely increases this to 70%, indicating that the CoT actively reinforces refusal. In a two-stage intervention where the model regenerates its CoT under activation steering, refusal is reversed in 94% of cases, while the resulting CoT alone retains 48% of this effect even after steering is removed. This suggests that the CoT can carry and reconstruct the compliance signal independently. These findings indicate that refusal in LRMs is jointly encoded in residual stream activations and CoT. This joint activation makes LRM more robust against activation-level interventions alone, but exposes CoT to a possible alternative surface attack.

</details>
